SETTLEMENT AGREEMENT AND RELEASE 
 
This SETTLEMENT AGREEMENT (the “Agreement”) is made and entered into this 
_____ day of ______________, 2023, by and between WJE, LLC, 2247 E. 19th St., Cheyenne, 
Wyoming 82001 (“WJE, LLC”), and the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming a municipality organized 
under the laws of the State of Wyoming, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, Wyoming, 82001 (the 
“City”), sometimes individually referred to herein as “Party” and collectively as “Parties.”  
 
WHEREAS, on April 6, 2023, through legal counsel, WJE, LLC filed with the City of 
Cheyenne’s City Clerk’s office a Notice of Governmental Claim ("Governmental Claim") pursuant 
to the Wyoming Governmental Claims Act, Wyoming Statute § 1-39-101 et seq. alleging the City 
of Cheyenne had taken real property from WJE, LLC during its construction of the Christiansen 
Road extension;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC’s Governmental Claim specifically alleged that the affected real 
property included approximately forty (40) acres of property at the northwest corner of the 
intersection of Pershing Boulevard and Christiansen Road;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC’s approved plat allowed for the construction of nineteen (19) home 
lots adjacent to the road in the Saddle Ridge addition, eighteenth (18th) filing;  
 
WHEREAS, during the construction of the Christiansen Road extension, WJE, LLC 
granted the City of Cheyenne a fifty (50) foot permanent and exclusive easement adjacent to WJE, 
LLC’s Saddle Ridge development;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC also granted the City of Cheyenne an additional thirty (30) foot 
temporary easement for construction of the Christiansen Road extension. WJE, LLC claimed that 
at the conclusion of the construction of the road, the property subject to the temporary construction 
easement would be returned to WJE, LLC for the development of the additional platted nineteen 
(19) homes in the Saddle Ridge addition, eighteenth (18th) filing;  
 
WHEREAS, when the City of Cheyenne completed the Christiansen Road extension, WJE, 
LLC claimed that the City of Cheyenne made dramatic and permanent changes to the land 
including removing a significant quantity of dirt/soil, which was relocated to other places;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC alleged that the removal of the soil/dirt altered the grade in the 
temporary easement area to approximately thirteen (13) feet lower then the rest of the WJE, LLC’s 
land resulting in a large amount of missing soil/dirt in the area of lands platting for the nineteen 
(19) lots;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC alleged that the City removed approximately forty two thousand, 
four hundred eighty-four and fifty four hundredths (42,484.54) cubic yards of soil/dirt from the 
temporary easement area. WJE, LLC further alleged that the value of the soil/dirt to be between 
three dollars and fifty cents ($3.50) to five dollars ($5.00) per cubic yard, depending on the distance 
replacement soil/dirt would have to be transported to a given site; 
 
4:44 pm, Nov 17 2023

Page 2 of 3 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC also alleged the City of Cheyenne appeared to consider the 
temporary easement area as part of the permanent right of way for Christiansen Road preventing 
the restoration of the thirty (30) foot temporary easement area for the construction of the additional 
nineteen (19) homes in the Saddle Ridge addition, eighteenth (18th) filing;  
 
WHEREAS, WJE, LLC also alleged that the value of the removal of the soil/direct and the 
altered grade resulted in a reduction in value of the affected real property in the amount of 
approximately two hundred fifty thousand dollars ($250,000.00);  
 
WHEREAS, although the City of Cheyenne has denied the allegations contained in WJE, 
LLC’s Governmental Claim, the City has been engaged in conversations with WJE, LLC in an 
attempt to resolve this dispute; and  
 
WHEREAS, the City of Cheyenne and WJE, LLC have reached a settlement agreement as 
set for the in more detail below. 
 
NOW, THEREFORE, in consideration of the mutual covenants and promises set forth in 
this Agreement, and for other good and valuable consideration, the adequacy and sufficiency of 
which are herby acknowledged by the parties, on behalf of City of Cheyenne and WJE, LLC the 
Settling Signatories agree as follows: 
 
1. 
EXECUTION DATE. As used in this Agreement, the “Execution Date” shall mean 
the date as of which the Agreement is fully executed by the Settling Signatories. 
 
2. 
SETTLEMENT AMOUNT.  In consideration of the release and dismissal of WJE, 
LLC’s claims against the City, including WJE, LLC's withdrawal of its Governmental Claim, the 
City agrees to allowing WJE, LLC to donate to the City the relevant  soil/dirt that was removed 
from WJE, LLC’s real property by the City and any diminution in value to real property owned by 
WJE, LLC related to the removal of the earth type materials from the temporary easement area 
referenced above, for an agreed value of two hundred fifty thousand dollars ($250,000.00) with 
the City to execute such documents as are necessary for WJE, LLC to claim such donation as a 
charitable donation with its filings with the Internal Revenue Service; 
 
3. 
The City and WJE, LLC agree that the temporary easement area is no longer in use 
by the City, and any fencing put up by the City to protect re-landscaping efforts in the temporary 
easement area or for other purposes, is not meant to change ownership of the real property so 
fenced, or claim any ownership by the City of the temporary easement real property area owned 
by WJE, LLC; 
 
4. 
WAIVER AND RELEASE OF LIABILITY.  In consideration for the settlement 
of WJE’S claims against the City of Cheyenne, WJE, LLC agrees to forever release, settle and 
discharge the City of Cheyenne from any and all claims, and liability of every nature, whether 
known or unknown, that concern, arise out of or relate in any way to the above-described 
Governmental Claim. 
 
5. 
ATTORNEYS’ FEES AND EXPENSES.  The Parties agree that the Settlement 

Page 3 of 3 
constitutes the entire payment to be made in settlement of WJE, LLC’s claims against the City in 
the Governmental Claim and WJE, LLC will not seek to recover additional amounts from the City 
including for its attorneys’ fees and expenses in connection with all matters related to the 
Governmental Claim and its settlement. Any fees and expenses to be paid to WJE, LLC’s counsel 
shall be paid solely by WJE, LLC. 
 
6. 
NO ADMISSION OF WRONGDOING.  This Agreement, the settlement reflected 
herein, and all communication or other actions relating to this settlement shall not be used by any 
Party for any purpose and shall not, in any way, be offered or construed as, or received against any 
of the Parties as evidence in any matter as an admission of wrongdoing on the part of the City. 
 
So agreed this ______ day of November, 2023 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
____________________________________ 
 
 
 
 
 
 
WJE, LLC 
 
 
 
__________________________________________ 
Pattrick Collins, Mayor 
City of Cheyenne 
 
(SEAL)

_____________________________________ 
Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk

RESOLUTION NO. _____________ 
ENTITLED: "A RESOLUTION TO PROVIDE SALARY ADJUSTMENTS TO 116 
NON-UNIFORMED EMPLOYEES IDENTIFIED THROUGH A 
WAGE MATRIX TOOL CREATED TO ADDRESS PAY DISPARITY." 
WHEREAS, City of Cheyenne employees are central for the successful delivery of City 
programs and services which enhance the quality of life for residents and contribute to the fiscal 
well-being of the City; 
WHEREAS, an equitable and fair pay structure which includes competitive salaries is 
essential in attracting and retaining a skilled and motivated work force; 
WHEREAS, consideration should be given to employees based on an appropriate 
combination of merit, experience, and regional competitiveness; 
WHEREAS, the 2023 market analysis provided by the Employers Council was used to 
create new minimum and maximum salary ranges for all non-uniformed positions throughout the 
City; 
WHEREAS, these new salary ranges created compression issues in various positions, 
sometimes causing tenured employees to earn the same or less compared to less tenured 
employees in the same or even higher-level position; 
WHEREAS, to address this disparity, a wage matrix tool was created that evaluates each 
employee's placement in their position's salary range based on a point system that focuses on the 
total number of years an employee has been with the City, with more points given for the number 
of years an employee has served in their current position; 
WHEREAS, an additional Human Resources Division wage matrix tool will help provide 
a structured approach to ensure that each employee's placement in their position's salary range is 
equitable and avoids further compression issues; and 
WHEREAS, this tool will be used by the Human Resources Division to evaluate 
appropriate starting wages for new employees and for employees who are promoted from within 
based on equivalent work experience and job position requirements. 
NOW, THEREFORE BE IT RESOLVED BY THE GOVERNING BODY OF THE 
CITY OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING, that one hundred sixteen (116) employees have been 
identified to receive adjustments effective December 1, 2023, using the new wage matrix tool in 
an annual amount not to exceed one hundred ninety-four thousand five hundred eighty-six 
dollars ($194,586) from the General Fund and seventy three thousand seven hundred fourteen 
dollars ($73,714) for other funds, plus applicable benefit costs. 
10:06 am, Nov 03 2023

PRESENTED, READ AND ADOPTED this _______ day of _____________________, 
2023. 
 
 
__________________________________________  
Patrick Collins, Mayor  
 
 
(SEAL)  
 
 
ATTEST: 
 
____________________________________  
Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk

MEMORANDUM 
TO: 
 
City of Cheyenne Governing Body  
FROM: 
 
Robin Lockman, City Treasurer 
DATE: 
 
November 7, 2023  
SUBJECT: 
Annual Write-Off Per Resolution 4970 
 
 
On November 13, 2023, the Governing Body will be referring a resolution to the Finance 
Committee to write off $14,576.90 in uncollectible accounts of the City and $47,639.76 for the 
Board of Public Utilities sanitation billings.  Per Resolution 4970, debts owed to the City that are 
at least four years old, the customer is deceased or their debt has been discharged in bankruptcy 
are considered “uncollectible”.   
The type of debt under consideration for write off is as follows: 
 
Please note that approval of this resolution does not prevent further collection efforts on the 
accounts listed.  The collection agency will keep working on these debts until the statute of 
limitations ends, even if the accounts are removed from our financial records.  However, it is a 
requirement under generally accepted accounting principles (GAAP) to recognize bad debt losses 
when they are reasonably certain to be uncollectible.  Otherwise, the City will have an inordinately 
high accounts receivable balance that overstates the amount of outstanding customer invoices that 
will eventually be converted into cash. 
As always, if you have any questions on the policies and procedures in place, please give me a call 
at 637-6336 or email at rlockman@cheyennecity.org.

RESOLUTION NO. __________ 
 
ENTITLED: "A RESOLUTION AUTHORIZING THE CITY TREASURER TO 
DISCHARGE UNCOLLECTIBLE DEBTS PURSUANT TO WYOMING 
STATUTE §16-4-502." 
 
WHEREAS, the City Treasurer and Board of Public Utilities Administration Manager are 
responsible for collecting debts owed to the City of Cheyenne using a process that includes internal 
efforts, legal remedies and the use of collection agencies; 
 
WHEREAS, once accounts reach a certain age without successful collection, generally 
accepted accounting principles (GAAP) require that accounts be written off the financial 
accounting records once management determines the receivable to be uncollectible after diligent 
and reasonable efforts were made to collect; 
 
WHEREAS, per Resolution 4970 the Governing Body deems debts owed to the City to be 
uncollectible that are four or more years old, the debtor is deceased, or the debt has been discharged 
in bankruptcy proceedings;  
 
WHEREAS, collection efforts such as liens filed by the City and accounts being sent to the 
City’s collection agency have failed;  
 
WHEREAS, Wyoming Statute § 16-4-502 provides for discharge of uncollectible debts 
upon proper certification to the Governing Body;  
 
WHEREAS, this certification includes the name of the debtor, a description of the goods 
or services for which the debt was incurred, the amount of the debt, the date when the debt became 
due and payable, and an explanation of what actions have been taken to collect the debt; 
 
WHEREAS, the City Treasurer has prepared the required report of uncollectible debts to 
be written off, a copy of which is attached to and incorporated as part of this Resolution; and 
 
NOW, THEREFORE BE IT RESOLVED BY THE GOVERNING BODY OF THE CITY 
OF CHEYENNE, WYOMING that the City Treasurer is authorized to discharge the City of 
Cheyenne’s uncollectible debts in the amount of fourteen thousand five hundred seventy-six 
dollars and ninety cents ($14,576.90) and the Board of Public Utilities sanitation debts in the 
amount of forty seven thousand six hundred thirty nine dollars and seventy six cents ($47,639.76). 
 
THIS RESOLUTION shall be in full force and effect from and after its passage in 
accordance with the laws of the State of Wyoming. 
 
 
 
 
 
8:21 am, Nov 07 2023

PRESENTED, READ AND ADOPTED this _______ day of _____________________, 
2023. 
 
 
__________________________________________  
Patrick Collins, Mayor  
 
 
(SEAL)

Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk
